Company profile 
Founded in 1986 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2007, Lotes is one of the world’s leading 
connector vendors specialising in CPU socket/ILM and connectors, such as DDR, PCIe, SATA, and 
Type-C, with most of its production capacities at its Guangzhou, Zhongshan and Suzhou plants. Its 
superior manufacturing capability and strong client relationships are the key differentiators that make 
Lotes stand out from its competitors. Since the PC market became saturated from 2015, Lotes has 
focused on penetrating non-PC connector businesses, such as connectors for servers, automotive and 
industrial, and new types of cables (eg, Thunderbolt cable).

### Page 5

5

Lotes (3533 TT): 13 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 13 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Lotes’ board consists of 7 directors, including 3 independent directors. The total number of
directors is slightly lower than other listed companies in our coverage. Lotes held 8 board
meetings in 2025 and the actual attendance rate was 95%.
Although the Chairman and CEO are not the same person, they are, in fact, brothers and the co-
founders of Lotes. It gives us a sense that Lotes is still a family enterprise, which is likely a
negative for the board and executive management. That said, the background of independent
directors is diversified with expertise in technology and management. We see it as a positive for
the board's quality.
Capital management 2
Lotes declared a DPS of TWD41.5 in 2025, representing a payout ratio of 50%. Being a leading
connector vendor for applications such as desktops, notebooks and servers, Lotes grew rapidly
along with its key clients (Intel and AMD) on strong technical capabilities and higher growth rate
from the server market. The solid revenue CAGR in the past decade required higher working
capital and continuous capex to expand its capacity, which could also be the culprit for its slightly
lower payout ratio compared with other electronic component makers. Having said that, we think
Lotes’ higher revenue growth should partially offset the drawback of a lower payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1
In 2025, there were no disclosed related-party sales, while purchases from related parties
amounted to TWD4.4m, representing 0.03% of total cost of sales. We see limited risk from
related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2
Lotes established its RBA management system in 2010 and requires its suppliers to meet the
standards of no child labour, no forced labour, treating employees with respect and dignity, and
caring for labourers' safety and health. Lotes hosts a yearly supplier conference to promote the
RBA guidelines to its suppliers. In 2025, 187 suppliers participated in the annual supplier
conference. Lotes also requires its suppliers to sign the RBA-related commitment letters. For
better supply chain management, Lotes conducts yearly audits and regular business reviews
and holds the yearly supplier conference as measures. In 2025, Lotes conducted written
evaluations of 110 suppliers and on-site audits of 61 suppliers. In addition, all 76 newly added
suppliers passed its environmental and social responsibility evaluations. Lotes has required new
suppliers to sign the "Supplier Social and Environmental Responsibility Commitment Letter"
since 2010 to extend social responsibility concepts and requirements to the supply chain.
For "green" purchase management, Lotes has adopted the IECQ QC 080000 hazardous
substance process management system. Lotes asks its suppliers to follow the RoHS, HSF, and
REACH guidelines to avoid using hazardous materials. Lotes also conducts conflict minerals due
diligence and requires suppliers to sign non-conflict mineral declarations and complete surveys
tracing mineral sources to smelters. Overall, Lotes believes it has constructed a comprehensive
system covering system certification (e.g., ISO 9001), product design, supply chain
management, and production techniques to ensure that the entire manufacturing process, from
raw materials to end products, follows green principles. All products from Lotes are now 100%
compliant with RoHS standards.
S Data security 2
Lotes has established a team to implement data security regulations, planning and maintenance.
Moreover, it monitors vulnerabilities in its information systems each year through regular
vulnerability scanning, access control reviews and information security audits. The company
reported no losses resulting from information security incidents in 2025.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Feedback
Strong demand with NO inventory build. Management reiterated that shipment
growth reflects solid demand, supported by expanding BMC adoption in networking,
power, and cooling infrastructure, rather thar inventory accumulation. To note, our
cloud semis downgrade is on 2028 capex uncertainty instead of inventory overbuild
(link).
Strong growth drivers into 2027. Accelerating AST2700 adoption and new

products (AST1080, AST1040 AST1840) should drive higher silicon content, more
comprehensive product mix, and sustained growth.
Pricing hike to protect margins. Aspeed plans price adjustments in 4Q to offset
rising manufacturing costs, prioritizing margin stability over expansion.
Security as an emerging opportunity. Integration of OCP Caliptra with eMemory
PUF technology should  strengthen Aspeed's hardware security capabilities and
support incremental content growth.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

We continue to see margin improvement, with October NPM at 23.4%, above
20.1% in 3Q26.
Margin improvement likely driven by combination of better pricing, improving

Stay OW on both Unimicron and NYPCB – but on a 12-month view, we prefer

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

公司拜訪快報

力旺(3529)：2H26 權利金大幅成長、Security 長期機會大 
建議：維持買進

一、2026 年前三季權利金營收
+13.4%YOY，PUF-based 權利
金+927%YOY 
 2026 前三季權利金營收 21.49 億元，+13.4%YOY。 
成熟製程 NeoBit 佔 24.6%，+8.6%YOY。 
先進製程 NeoFuse 佔 70.7%，+12.3%YOY。 
PUF-based 佔 1.5%，+927%YOY。 
MTP 佔 3.2%，+32.5%YOY。 
權利金成長預計將持續加速，主要受惠於先進製程應用的 ASP 提升、客戶新產
品持續放量、PUF 相關部署的營收貢獻增加，以及高權利金費率應用的產品組
合比重提高。

二、2026 年前三季授權金
+38.2% YOY，PUF - based 
+234% YOY 
 2026 前三季授權金營收 12.51 億元，+38.2%YOY。 
NeoBit 佔 11.9%，-39.3%YOY。 
NeoFuse 佔 36.5%，+35.1%YOY。 
PUF-based 佔 31.7%，+234%YOY。 
MTP 佔 19.9%，+22.9%YOY。 
授權業務預期將維持強勁的成長動能，主要受惠於先進與成熟製程節點、硬體安
全、AI 基礎設施，以及次世代快閃記憶體 Flash 應用等領域。

三、維持買進理由  1) 受惠於先進製程應用的 ASP 提升、客戶新產品持續放量、PUF 相關部署的
營收貢獻增加，以及高權利金費率應用的產品組合比重提高，權利金成長預計將
持續加速。 
2) 授權業務預期將維持強勁的成長動能，主要受惠於先進與成熟製程節點、硬
體安全、AI 基礎設施，以及次世代快閃記憶體 Flash 應用等領域。 
3) PUF-based 已貢獻 2026 年前三季營收 12.6%，+252%YOY。應用面包括
CPU、Storage、光通、FPGA、高速傳輸晶片多面向。Arm Neoverse CSS V3
已將力旺的 PUF 列入核心元件。

訪談速報 
貿聯-KY(3665) 
毛利重返成長軌道 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $2,200 
前次投資建議：2026.5.13： 
「逢低買進」，目標價: $3,300

報告日期：2026.11.16 研究員：陳泂暉

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/13 收盤價： 1,520.00 毛利重返成長軌道，上調投資評等至「買進」 
目前股價 12X27(F)PER，考量： 
(1) 26Q3 公 司 營 收 為 278.54 億元，QoQ+19.6% 、
YoY+51.3%，受惠 HPC 電源需求、SPE 沉積蝕刻及次
系統提升與電氣化推進；毛利率為 32.6% ，
QoQ+1.9ppts、YoY-0.7ppts，費用率創低帶動營益率創
高；稅後淨利 42.78億元，QoQ+43.5%、YoY+62.8%；
稅後 EPS 21.93元。 
(2) 工業業務受惠工廠自動化回溫及能源、電動車等電氣化
需求推進，機器人、航太與醫療等客製化方案亦逐步貢
獻。半導體設備受惠沉積與蝕刻滲透率提升，客戶投資
看至 2030年後，帶動子系統整合與機箱組裝委外需求擴
大。 
(3) HPC受惠 AI基礎設施升級，機櫃內銅纜與機櫃外光纖將
長期共存。公司透過 XFS 強化光學封裝與量產，推進
CPO、12.8T XPO 及 Micro-VCSEL 技術；電源延伸至
Busbars、匯流排槽及水冷板等全液冷機櫃方案，結合
Interplex 機構整合並共同開發 LVDC/HVDC 架構，推升
系統級競爭力。 
(4) 預估 2027 年營收 1,636.24 億元，YoY+54.4%，受惠於
新世代平台放量；毛利率 33.6%，YoY+2.1ppts，受惠於
產品組合改善；稅後淨利 256.69 億元，YoY+74.6%；稅
後 EPS 131.58元。

貿聯目前股價位於 12X27(F)PER，位於公司過往 9-34X區間
下緣位置，考量公司持續推進光通業務，將有助於評價回升，
所以取 17X，給予「買進」投資評等， 6 個月目標價
2,200(17X2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 44.74%。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估

訪談重點

全球連接線束解決方案領導者 
貿聯成立於 1996 年，總部位於加州矽谷，註冊於開曼群島，為全球連接線
束解決方案的領導廠商。產品分為工業用、電器用、車用、資訊科技與其他
五大產線。主要應用於資訊科技用線、電腦週邊設備、數據中心用線、消費
電子用線、電器用線、車用線、醫療設備用線、光通訊設備、太陽能連接裝
置、工業設備用線、半導體設備等線材、連接器等領域。公司 26Q1 產品營
收比重為: 工業用 31%、電器用 12%、車用 8%、資訊科技 48%。

HPC 與半導體設備雙引擎驅動營益率創歷史新高 
26Q3 公司營收為 278.54 億元，QoQ+19.6%、YoY+51.3%，細分產品組合
來看，HPC 高效能運算受惠於 AI 基礎設施持續投資，其中電源互連因高電
流與機櫃級配電需求強勁、旗下 XFS光學業務月營收運行率自年初以來已超
過翻倍、SPE半導體生產設備受惠於沉積與蝕刻等設備平台滲透率及次系統
承接比重提升，並持續大幅優於整體 WFE 市場、其他業務整體市場環境好
壞參半，但電氣化與自動化需求持續推進，另併購之 Interplex於 26Q3 僅計
入 7天營收，10月起完整併表並單月貢獻 6,700萬美元；毛利率為 32.6%，
QoQ+1.9ppts、YoY-0.7ppts，搭配營業費用率降至歷史新低，帶動營業利益
率創下歷史新高；稅後淨利 42.78 億元，QoQ+43.5%、YoY+62.8%；稅後
EPS 21.93元。

動能從單一零組件邁向子系統級解決方案，動能延續至 2027年後 
工業用業務方面，受惠工廠自動化需求活動持續改善，加上受惠於電氣化與
基礎設施現代化的能源、電動車及特用工程應用穩健推進，帶動整體表現持
續成長；此外，公司在機器人、航太、海事、醫療科技、半導體量測及儲能
等領域的客製化線束與整合方案，部分已成為穩定貢獻來源，部分則處於早
期認證與商業開發階段。

半導體生產設備方面，主要受惠於沉積與蝕刻設備平台滲透率與內含價值提
升，且客戶針對高複雜度製程的投資預期強勁並延伸至 2030 年以後，帶動
高複雜度組裝、子系統製造與整合、測試及機箱組裝的委外需求持續擴大。
隨著各產業客戶對供應商的要求從單一零組件轉向具備工程支援、產品認

### Page 3

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）； 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）； 區間
操作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）； 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出
（預期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。

- 3 - 
訪談速報 
證、量產準備度與穩定執行力的子系統級解決方案，結合併購 Interplex所帶
來的精密機構與機殼整合綜效，相關成長動能預期將延續至 2027 年及更長
遠的基礎設施與產能擴張週期。

機櫃內銅纜、機櫃外光纖並進，提供完整互連方案 
公司持續受惠於高速銅互連解決方案在支援更高頻寬與維持訊號完整性上的
優勢；隨著 AI系統頻寬需求、光纖密度與互連複雜度提高，光互連基礎設施
的重要性持續提升，同時銅互連在機櫃內短距離傳輸、成本與部署特性上仍
具效率優勢，因此光互連不會單向取代銅互連，而是機櫃內以銅纜為主、機
櫃外長距離傳輸以光纖為主，兩者將依頻寬、傳輸距離、功耗與封裝架構不
同而長期共存。公司透過旗下 XFS Communications 補強精密光學組裝、連
接器化製程、高密度封裝與快速量產擴張能力，並陸續於 OFC、Computex
與 OCP展出光纖方案、CPO交換器托盤及 12.8T XPO收發方案，同時推進
次世代基於 Micro-VCSEL的機櫃內擴展（Scale-up）光學互連技術與外部技
術合作，成為後續 HPC業務擴張的重要動能。

在 HPC端，AI基礎設施進入長期投資與升級週期，隨著算力密度持續攀升，
資料中心的物理基礎設施挑戰正延伸至高電流電力傳輸、散熱及系統級整
合。公司產品線已從單一電源線延伸至 Busbars、Power Whips、連接器、
匯流排槽以及水冷板、分歧管、液冷快接頭與漏液偵測等全液冷 AI 機櫃方
案，並結合併購 Interplex的精密機構與機殼整合能力強化系統級競爭力；至
於低壓直流與高壓直流新電源架構的導入管理層指出將取決於各家客戶的系
統設計、產品認證進度及資料中心電力基礎設施的就緒程度，目前公司正與
客戶密切共同開發次世代與下下世代架構，以高度彈性配合其部署排程。

新世代平台放量帶動獲利爆發 
預估 26Q4營收 339.62億元，QoQ+21.9%、YoY+71.7%，因為工業用季節
性需求回溫，以及 HPC 新平台所帶動；毛利率 32.8%，QoQ+0.2ppts、
YoY+0.9ppts，因新平台導入，因此壓抑毛利率成長幅度；稅後淨利 51.7億
元，QoQ+20.9%、YoY+88.7%；稅後 EPS 26.5元。

預估 2026年營收 1,059.61億元，YoY+48.7%，持續受惠於資料中心業務；
毛利率 31.5%，YoY-0.2ppts；稅後淨利 147.04 億元，YoY+63.3%；稅後
EPS 75.37元。

預估 2027年營收 1,636.24 億元，YoY+54.4%，受惠於新世代平台放量；毛
利率 33.6%，YoY+2.1ppts，受惠於產品組合改善；稅後淨利 256.69 億元，
YoY+74.6%；稅後 EPS 131.58元。

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 비현금성 비용 항목 개선으로 시장 기대치
를 상회했으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인플레이션 등으로 연간 가이던스는 
하향되었습니다. 올해 생산 차질에 따라 이연된 신차 판매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시화하는 부분에 주목 필요합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제외하면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년으로 이연된 것이라는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본격화된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로컬업체들의 공세를 방어하는 모습을 
확인하는 것이 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 노후차종 중심 운영에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근원적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급

 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI 로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000 원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 2,000,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.21) 
1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

산업 투자의견 
- Overweight(비중확대) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 초과 상승 예상 
- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 하회 예상

기업 투자의견 
- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 -10%p~10%p 주가 변동 예상 
- Underperform(시장수익률 하회)

Document 279: AI交易大跌后，汇丰：科技股已在定价“最坏情景”，2027年或迎“现金流翻身仗”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易大跌后，汇丰：科技股已在定价“最坏情景”，2027年或迎“现金流翻身仗”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

汇丰认为， 2026 年全球股市尤其是 AI 板块的深度回调已将估值压⾄ “ 最坏情景 ” 。随着云⼚
商⾃由现⾦流将于 2027 年上半年⻅底回升，叠加 AI 变现担忧、美债收益率及能源价格 “ 三⼤
⻅顶 ” ， 2027 年股市⻛险回报⽐显著改善，标普 500 每股盈利预计增⻓ 17% 。
摘要：
汇丰认为，全球股市尤其是 AI 板块在 2026 年的深度回撤，已经把估值压到 “ 最坏情景 ” 的⽔平。随
着超⼤规模云⼚商⾃由现⾦流在 2027 年⼀季度或⼆季度⻅底回升、债券收益率⻅顶以及美联储
紧缩⼒度不及预期， 2027 年股市的⻛险回报⽐已显著改善。
据追⻛交易台消息，这家投⾏的全球股票策略主管 Alastair Pinder 在 11 ⽉ 24 ⽇发布的《 2027 年
全球股票展望》中表⽰，标普 500 指数每股盈利 2027 年预计增⻓ 17% ，全球科技股当前估值所隐
含的中期 EPS 增速已接近零，⽽ 2025 年中期这⼀数字还是 25% 。他判断，只要此前压制估值的

三⼤压⼒不再加剧，市场就有望迎来重估。
这⼀判断的核⼼催化剂是现⾦流拐点。汇丰预计，随着云收⼊加速和资本开⽀上调放缓，美国
超⼤规模云⼚商的⾃由现⾦流将在 2027 年上半年⻅底，这被视为打消 “AI 变现 ” 疑虑的关键信
号。与此同时，市场已计⼊美联储到 2027 年中期再加息 65 个基点，汇丰认为进⼀步偏鹰的空间
有限。
在配置上，汇丰超配美国和新兴市场，中性看待欧洲，低配⽇本；⾏业层⾯偏好科技尤其是半
导体，同时看多⾦融、⼯业和材料等周期板块。该⾏强调，在盈利增速集中、 AI ⽣态循环性上升
的背景下，资产负债表质量⽐以往更重要。
估值已计⼊ “ 最坏情景 ”
2026 年全球股市的下跌主要集中在估值端。据汇丰统计， MSCI 全球基准指数（ FTSE All-
World ）的 12 个⽉远期市盈率今年以来下降 25% ，标普 500 的远期市盈率较 2025 年末⾼点下滑
28% ，近 20% 的成分股估值收缩超过 30% ，另有 15% 收缩 20% ⾄ 30% 。
根据其折现盈利模型倒推，标普 500 当前估值所隐含的中期（ FY2 ⾄ FY5 ） EPS 增速已从 2025 年

中期的 16% 复合增速降⾄ 7% ，⼤致回到⼗年均值附近；全球科技股隐含的中期增速则从 25% 骤
降⾄ 2% 左右。汇丰称，从估值⻆度看，市场已在为 “ 最坏情景 ” 定价。
回撤的⼴度同样显著。新兴市场个股平均较 52 周⾼点低 35% ，为 2010 年以来最深的回撤之⼀；
标普 500 个股平均下跌 22% 。美国 10 年期国债收益率升⾄约 5% ，为 2007 年以来最⾼。
汇丰称，估值每进⼀步上⾏ 50 个基点，合理市盈率就会被压缩 4.3% 。不过，其固收团队认为利
率或已⻅顶，预计到 2027 年底美国 10 年期收益率为 4.75% 。
三⼤ “ ⻅顶 ” 构成重估催化剂
汇丰提出， 2027 年将出现三个 “ ⻅顶 ” ： AI 变现担忧⻅顶、债券收益率⻅顶、能源价格⻅顶。这
三者同时转向，是推动估值重估的基础。
在 AI ⽅⾯，市场此前的担忧集中在基础设施供给增速快于可⽀撑的收⼊。据汇丰统计，市场对
2026 年⾄ 2030 年超⼤规模云⼚商资本开⽀的⼀致预期合计达 5.5 万亿美元，年均 1.1 万亿美元，

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

另有 2.5 万亿美元的未开始租赁和采购承诺。该⾏认为， 2027 年将证明 AI 需求之强以及这些资本
开⽀的变现能⼒。
其依据包括：前沿模型需求依然强劲，⽽企业 AI ⽀出仍处低位。据路透 10 ⽉ 8 ⽇报道， Anthropic
最新 IPO ⽂件显⽰，其 10 ⽉年化收⼊运⾏率达到 810 亿美元； OpenAI 在 9 ⽉达到 500 亿美元。
Ramp 数据显⽰，企业 AI ⽀出中位数为每名员⼯每⽉ 16.3 美元，较 5 ⽉增⻓ 60% ，⽽⽀出最⾼的
10% 企业为 800 美元，最⾼的 1% 为 1 万美元。
汇丰测算，若标普 500 企业平均按每名员⼯每⽉ 800 美元⽀出，年⽀出将达 3050 亿美元，相当于
标普 500 EBITDA 的 6.2% 、⼯资总额的 10% 、研发⽀出的 33% 。据 Cledara 数据， AI ⽬前仅占软
件预算的 25% ，却获得新增 IT 及软件预算的 50% 。汇丰科技团队预计，到 2030 年 AI ⼤模型⽣态
年收⼊将达到约 9200 亿美元。

超⼤规模⼚商：现⾦流拐点是最重要催化剂
汇丰认为，美国超⼤规模云⼚商拥有该板块中最强的商业模式。它们既从算⼒供给中获取收
⼊，⼜从平台上的模型调⽤中抽取费⽤。 Anthropic 的 S-1 ⽂件显⽰，其平台交易抽成约 16% ，且
不计算⼒成本。
算⼒定价显⽰需求仍超过供给。尽管产能快速扩张，英伟达 B200 和 H100 的租赁价格仍在上涨。
微软、亚⻢逊、 Alphabet 和甲⻣⽂的剩余履约义务（ RPO ）在 2026 年三季度达到 2.5 万亿美元，
⾼于 2025 年⼆季度的 8150 亿美元。市场预计，亚⻢逊、微软和 Alphabet 合计云收⼊将从 2026 年
⼆季度年化 4000 亿美元，增⾄ 2028 年四季度近 9250 亿美元。
资本开⽀上调已开始放缓。最近⼀个财报季中，超⼤规模⼚商 2027 年资本开⽀⼀致预期上调 550
亿美元，上⼀季度为 1450 亿美元。汇丰认为，上调幅度的峰值已经过去，这意味着⾃由现⾦流
预测⾯临的下⾏压⼒将减轻，市场⼀致预期已指向 2027 年⼀季度或⼆季度现⾦流拐点。

该⾏同时提⽰了数据中⼼的政治阻⼒。⺠主党在得州和俄亥俄州胜选后，汇丰估算，美国已规
划或在建的 310 吉⽡数据中⼼容量中，约 80% 位于⺠主党州⻓主政的州，中期选举前为 35% 。不
过，该⾏认为选举不太可能阻断建设，更可能的结果是审批时间拉⻓、并⽹成本更多由云⼚商
承担。得州当选州⻓ Gina Hinojosa 表⽰，其提出的暂停令仅适⽤于仍在等待州许可的项⽬。
Meta 总裁在选举后不久称，已有⼀位即将上任的州⻓要求会⾯，讨论留住本州的数据中⼼投资
（据《华盛顿观察家报》 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇报道）。

### Page 3

半导体：估值⼤幅重置，盈利可⻅性更好
在科技板块内部，汇丰继续偏好受益于资本开⽀周期的 “ 卖铲⼈ ” 。据彭博供应链数据， 2027 年超
⼤规模⼚商资本开⽀中近 78% 将流向半导体和 AI 服务器。经历近期抛售后，美国半导体股对应
12 个⽉远期盈利仅 9.8 倍，⾃ 6 ⽉以来估值收缩 60% 。
汇丰认为，半导体企业直接受益于训练和推理量的增⻓，收⼊对单⼀模型或应⽤的最终成败依
赖较低，叠加资本开⽀计划仍处⾼位，其盈利可⻅性优于许多最终承担变现任务的公司。
存储⽅⾯，汇丰认为估值已在计⼊盈利快速周期性下滑。盈利动能虽仍为正但已开始放缓。但
该⾏认为短期威胁被⾼估：⻓期协议的普及提供了远⾼于以往周期的下⾏保护。
以美光为例，其已签署 26 份照付不议协议，覆盖到 2030 年预期收⼊的 35% 以上。其中四分之三
的收⼊已确定定价，主要通过价格上下限设定； 16 份协议中有 14 份对应约 1000 亿美元的最低价
格累计收⼊。管理层预计，在价格下限情形下⽑利率仍将超过以往周期峰值。汇丰称， DRAM 价

格是近期最关键的指标，在存储股下跌期间始终坚挺，尚未出现估值所预期的价格疲软。
AI 采⽤率提速，盈利上修已现
汇丰预计，随着更多企业在利润率与销售增⻓中体现⽣产⼒收益， AI 采⽤将在 2027 年成为投资
主题。其基于财报电话会议样本的统计显⽰，美国约 80% 的企业已在讨论 AI 的实际⽤途，中国⼤
陆采⽤率也相对较⾼。
其数据科学团队⽤ GPT-5 对财报电话会议中的 AI 表述打分， 1 分仅停留在概念层⾯， 5 分需有可
量化的收益证据。美国企业中仅 20% 达到这⼀较⾼⻔槛，但⽐例在快速上升。
在盈利上修上已出现分化。汇丰将评分为 4 分或 5 分的企业归为 “AI 采⽤者 ” ，这些企业 2026 年
EPS 预测的上修幅度在美国、欧洲和⽇本分别⾼出其余样本 2 、 1.5 和 1 个百分点，主要来⾃利润
率预测上调。
该⾏列⽰的案例包括：美国西⽅⽯油称，对约 9000 ⼝井的 AI 监测使年产量最⾼提升 7% 、故障率
下降 22% ；欧洲诺基亚称其 AI ⽹络⾃动化⼯具将⽹络中断时间削减 96% ；新兴市场中通快递

称， 2026 年上半年运输成本节约中约 10% 归功于 AI ；⽇本索尼称，过去五年 AI ⽤于 PlayStation
⽀付路由带来超过 7 亿美元的增量收⼊。
盈利： 2027 年增⻓ 17% ，但集中度是⻛险
汇丰预计 2027 年盈利仍将稳健。市场⼀致预期标普 500 2027 年 EPS 增⻓ 17% ，低于 2026 年的
32% ，但后者包含微软、 Alphabet 和亚⻢逊对 Anthropic 与 OpenAI 投资的⼀次性重估收益 ——

### Page 4

2026 年上半年这三家公司其他净收⼊合计因此增加 2000 亿美元。剔除这⼀因素， 2027 年盈利增
速将接近 25% 。
增⻓⾼度集中。仅英伟达和美光两家就预计贡献标普 500 EPS 增⻓的约 50% ，汇丰直⾔这本⾝
就是⻛险。此外，对英伟达的独⽴调查和国会审查可能导致出⼝限制收紧。
除集中度外，其余市场增⻓依然稳健。标普 500 成分股 EPS 增速中位数预计约 10% ，与 2026 年⼤
致持平。⽇本 2027 年盈利预计增⻓ 17% ， MSCI 新兴市场指数（不含韩国）预计增⻓ 16% ，欧洲
约为 10% 。
配置：超配美国与新兴市场，偏好周期与⾼质量
区域配置上，汇丰超配美国，理由是盈利领先；超配新兴市场，理由是外资流出创纪录后估值
吸引⼒上升；中性看待欧洲，其盈利仍有韧性但增⻓落后，且相对美国的估值折价已收窄，不
过该⾏看好欧洲的⾦融和⼯业等周期板块；低配⽇本，其盈利增⻓仍强但相对其他国际市场偏
贵，且⽇本央⾏若⽐预期更鸽派，可能令⽇元承压。
⾏业上，汇丰超配科技、⾦融、⼯业和材料，低配必需消费、电信和公⽤事业，对可选消费、
医疗、能源和房地产持中性。该⾏表⽰，增⻓仍具韧性，因此偏向周期股，并更看好⼯业与材
料⽽⾮消费板块；在 2027 年前期尤其偏好资产负债表强劲的公司。

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 283: AI信用危机、美国经济硬着陆？美银Hartnett预判2027年两大意外

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI信用危机、美国经济硬着陆？美银Hartnett预判2027年两大意外.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

美国银⾏⾸席投资策略师哈特奈特建议， 2027 年上半年优先保护本⾦，待全球加息周期在
春季结束后，再布局芯⽚、⼤宗商品和消费股。其基准情景是全球经济软着陆，但 AI 投资
引发信⽤危机和美国经济硬着陆构成两⼤⻛险，极端情况下标普 500 指数或在 8888 点与
6666 点之间波动。
摘要：
2027 年全球市场可能迎来哪些意外？美国银⾏⾸席投资策略师 Michael Hartnett （迈克尔 · 哈特奈
特）建议投资者上半年暂缓加仓⻛险资产，等待全球加息周期在明年春季结束后，再转向周期
性板块。
在其基准情景中，全球经济有望实现软着陆，周期性板块将成为下半年投资主线。但市场也⾯
临两⼤尾部⻛险： AI 投资热潮引发信⽤危机，以及美国经济意外硬着陆。
具体⽽⾔，哈特奈特预计，全球央⾏到明年 6 ⽉前仍将累计加息 38 次，下半年才会出现约 21 次降
息。这意味着 2027 年市场可能先经历⼀段货币紧缩压⼒，再迎来政策转向带来的配置机会。他
建议投资者在 3 ⽉⾄ 4 ⽉前优先保护本⾦，待加息周期结束后重点布局芯⽚、⼤宗商品、消费股

等资产。
对于极端情景，他给出了两个截然不同的标普 500 指数⽬标：如果 AI 繁荣持续且政策⽀持⼒度超
预期，指数可能冲上 8888 点；如果美国经济意外硬着陆，则可能跌⾄ 6666 点。
上半年先防御：全球加息周期尚未结束
Hartnett 预计，全球央⾏到明年 6 ⽉前仍将累计加息 38 次，下半年则可能累计降息 21 次。加息与
降息在时间上的错位，意味着上半年通胀压⼒和货币紧缩仍将制约⻛险资产表现。
因此，他建议投资者暂缓⼤举配置⻛险资产，在 3 ⽉⾄ 4 ⽉前以保护本⾦为优先，保留⾜够的投
资弹药。
不过， Hartnett 对全球股市的中期前景并不悲观。其基准情景是全球经济实现软着陆，全球企业
盈利增⻓约 10% ，低于市场普遍预期，但仍有望推动全球股市上涨 4% ⾄ 8% 。
估值也并⾮主要障碍。⽬前 MSCI 全球全市值指数市盈率约为 14 倍，剔除美国后约为 11 倍，均低
于过去⼗年的平均⽔平。 Hartnett 认为，估值已不再构成明显阻碍，但在加息周期结束前，尚不

⾜以⽀持全⾯进攻。
AI 繁荣背后，信⽤⻛险可能引爆熊市
Hartnett 重点警⽰ AI 投资热潮背后的资⾦压⼒。 AI 相关企业正⼤举建设数据中⼼和基础设施，⾏
业资本⽀出规模逼近 1.3 万亿美元，但相关项⽬产⽣的⾃由现⾦流预计为负 2830 亿美元。
如果巨额投⼊迟迟⽆法转化为实际收⼊和营业利润，企业融资压⼒可能迅速上升，最终演变为
AI 驱动的信⽤事件。数据中⼼和芯⽚⾏业⼀旦出现融资问题，半导体价格⼤幅下跌可能进⼀步
冲击整个科技板块。
与之相对，极端⽜市情景需要多项利好同时兑现。 Hartnett 认为，如果 AI 繁荣持续，美国政策层
进⼀步加⼤⽀持⼒度，标普 500 指数有望冲上 8888 点。⼀种可能的政治情景是，⺠主党⽀持政府

Document 334: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-26).pdf

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

Document 445: The US tariff wall keeps changing, but it isn’t coming down

Source PDF: all report pdf/The US tariff wall keeps changing, but it isn’t coming down.pdf

### Page 1

The US is preparing another round of tariﬀs, this time targeting
structural overcapacity across 16 trading partners. It is another
reminder that while the legal justiﬁcation for tariﬀs keeps
changing, US protectionism is becoming increasingly diﬃcult to
unwind
Inga Fechner
The US is preparing new tariﬀs targeting structural overcapacity. Legal routes
may change, but the US tariﬀ wall is proving hard to dismantle
If anyone thought the Supreme Court ruling earlier this year

marked the beginning of the end of the US tariﬀ wall, think again.
The Trump administration is reportedly preparing new tariﬀs
following its Section 301 investigation into structural excess
capacity. China is the obvious target, but the list of 16 economies
also includes the European Union, Japan, South Korea, Mexico, India
and several Southeast Asian manufacturing hubs. The rates and
exact product coverage remain unknown, but the direction does
not.

### Page 2

We've argued for some time that tariﬀs are here to stay. In our
2025 trade analysis, we expected that even successful trade
negotiations and legal challenges would not signiﬁcantly bring
down the overall tariﬀ level, as Washington had other legal
instruments available. And that is pretty much what happened.
When the Supreme Court struck down tariﬀs imposed under IEEPA,
Washington initially turned to Section 122 as a temporary solution
before replacing it with broader Section 301 tariﬀs in July. Section

232 tariﬀs have also expanded considerably, with a growing
number of product groups now facing additional duties ranging
from 10% to as much as 100%, and more could follow.
Investigations into anthracite coal and medical equipment remain
open, while robotics, industrial machinery and wind turbines have
already moved to the negotiation stage, with tariﬀs still on the
table.
Section 232 tariffs already cover major
industries
Tariﬀ ranges reﬂect diﬀerences by product and/or country of origin.

Minimum import prices for polysilicon and derivatives will also apply
from 4 December 2026.

### Page 3

In turn, there is no uniform tariﬀ, but rather a complex system of
country- and sector-speciﬁc duties based on diﬀerent legal
authorities. According to the ETRX US Eﬀective Tariﬀ Rate Index,
which is based on US Census Bureau trade data, the eﬀective tariﬀ
rate stood at 6.8% for all US imports in September. For imports from
China, it was around 21%, compared with 6.6% for imports from
the EU.
That gap partly reﬂects the much broader range of tariﬀs on

Chinese goods, including Section 301 duties dating back to US
President Donald Trump's ﬁrst administration. Both China and the
EU are also exposed to newer Section 301 tariﬀs and relevant
sector-speciﬁc Section 232 measures. The next overcapacity tariﬀs
would add another legal instrument to this increasingly complex
tariﬀ system, although it remains unclear how they would interact
with current tariﬀs and agreed country-speciﬁc caps. According to
Reuters, Washington is expected to respect the 15% tariﬀ cap

agreed with the EU, while the 20% cap on Trump's second-term
tariﬀs on China would remain in place as long as the current US-
China truce is extended beyond 10 January 2027.
Overcapacity: the next justiﬁcation
Structural overcapacity is the latest justiﬁcation, and it is deﬁned
broadly. USTR points not only to subsidies, but also to state-owned
enterprises, subsidised lending, market-access barriers, wages,
currency practices and environmental and labour standards. And

the potential sectoral reach is equally broad, stretching from steel,
cars and batteries to semiconductors, machinery, chemicals and
solar panels. That gives Washington considerable room to
introduce new tariﬀs.
Another legal fallback?
And another legal route for tariﬀs could matter more than ever. The
10-12.5% Section 301 tariﬀs introduced in July against 60 trading
partners over their failure to impose and eﬀectively enforce
prohibitions on imports of goods produced with forced labour are

being challenged before the US Court of International Trade.
Plaintiﬀs argue, among other things, that the measures are too

The court has heard verbal arguments, and a ruling remains
pending.
We don't know how that case will end, and importantly, the two

investigations were launched almost simultaneously in March –
overcapacity on 11 March and forced labour on 12 March. But with
the forced-labour tariﬀs now facing a legal challenge, the
overcapacity investigation could oﬀer Washington another route to
maintain part of the tariﬀ wall should the current tariﬀs be struck
down or narrowed.
Different politics, same direction
Democrats taking control of Congress in January will put greater
pressure on Trump's tariﬀs. In theory, Congress has considerable

power over trade. It could try to restrict presidential tariﬀ authority,
remove individual tariﬀs and use hearings and investigations to
scrutinise the administration's trade policy. In practice, though,
actually reversing Trump's tariﬀs will be diﬃcult. Congress has
delegated substantial powers to the executive under laws such as
Sections 232 and 301. Curtailing those powers would require
legislation, which Trump could veto. Without the two-thirds

majority needed to override a presidential veto, Democrats' ability
to dismantle the tariﬀ wall will remain limited.
Either way, we don't think the blue wave will mark a return to free
trade. The Biden years already showed that tariﬀs in strategically
important sectors enjoy support across political camps.
Protectionism may become more targeted, but neither it nor the
tariﬀ wall is likely to disappear.

Document 471: When tokenization meets securitized debt markets (2026-11-17)

### Page 2

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
When tokenization meets securitized debt markets
Asset tokenization continues to grow in 2026. Excluding stablecoins, the
market grew from ~$10bn in Jan 2025 to ~$40bn in Nov 2026. Including
tokens confined to closed or permissioned environments, the total reached
~$420bn (here).
This chartbook explores tokenization’s potential in securitized markets, where

complex structuring, long settlement times and high transaction costs make
the asset class a strong candidate for tokenization.
Today, tokenization mainly improves securities-market infrastructure by
streamlining intermediation, cutting loan origination costs and speeding up
institutional securitization. US-based companies Figure Technologies,
Centrifuge and Maple Finance illustrate the benefits.
The transformative end state combines tokenized infrastructure (represented

tokenization) with tokens transferable outside the issuing platform (distributed
tokenization). Only when both are combined could tokenization reduce credit
risk, deepen the investor base, speed up the flow of capital, and lead to a more
robust, liquid US securitized market.
In this chartbook, we: (i) explain securitization and its challenges; (ii) examine
tokenization’s potential benefits; and (iii) outline the steps needed for
widespread adoption.

….but only ~$8bn in distributed value.
Sources: Deutsche Bank Research, RWA.xyz. Data as of Nov 12, 2026. Credit combines asset-backed credit,
corporate credit, diversified credit and specialty finance. “Represented” refers to tokens using the blockchain
as a recordkeeping layer, enhancing transparency, reconciliation, and operational efficiency without enabling
onchain investor transfer or distribution.
Sources: Deutsche Bank Research, RWA.xyz. Data as of Nov 13, 2026. “Distributed”

refers to tokens transferable outside the issuing platform and between wallets; it does
not measure trading activity.
Tokenized credit’s total value is ~$50bn in November, with Figure’s HELOC
token representing $26trn…

### Page 3

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
A ~$15trn market financing housing, consumers and companies
❑ The securitized (structured credit) market includes residential real estate
(RMBS), commercial real estate (CMBS), consumer borrowing (ABS –
autos, credit cards, student loans), and corporate debt (CLOs).
❑ It is a material part of the US growth engine: ~$15trn of debt outstanding,
roughly half of 2025 US nominal GDP. About 75% is linked to US housing

market.
❑ This market emerged in the late 1970s, when the US economy struggled
with sluggish growth and high inflation from elevated energy costs.
❑ Securitization was seen as financial innovation: It let bank lenders move
debt off their balance sheets and syndicate it to public markets as a
pooled asset.
❑ In the three decades before the GFC, the market’s growth increased
capital velocity in US debt markets while simultaneously reducing
financing costs for both borrowers and originators.

❑ Securitization produces pricing efficiency by bundling a diversified pool
of assets with consistent cashflows (e.g., such as corporate loans or
monthly mortgage payments) into a security held by a bankruptcy remote
special purpose vehicle (SPV).
❑ These pooled assets are then sliced into “tranches” with varying credit
risk and corresponding levels of yield, sold to institutional investors (life
insurers to hedge funds) that find relative value in the structure and

diversification.
Residential mortgages (RMBS) plays an outsized role in the market
The US Securitized market is approaching $16trn in market size
Sources: SIFMA, Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research.

### Page 4

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
Securitization is held back by complexity, slow settlement and high costs
A CLO investor can expect their first coupon payment typically 6 months
after a deal’s pricing date
Although securitization is deeply embedded into the US financial
system, several challenges remain:
❑ Inherent complexity: Collateralization complicates ownership
tracking, especially as deals are upsized, widely distributed and

traded in a secondary market. This was visible during the GFC, and in
recent pockets of credit stress – the tracking of collateral and of
asset ownership can present logistical hurdles over the lifetime of an
asset.
❑ Long settlement times: Complex structuring can delay settlement
and prolong the liquidity gap between allocating capital to a deal and
when the first coupon distribution takes place.  For example, a new
issue CLO can take up to two months from pricing to close, with an

additional four months before the first coupon distribution date takes
place.
❑ High transaction costs: Intermediaries between the buyer and seller
provide essential services but increase costs when issuing a deal.
Additionally, the ongoing deal administration from monthly collateral
monitoring to coupon distributions adds an additional layer of
logistical complexity to a securitization post close. The key
stakeholders required in this value chain range from arranging banks,

trustees, rating agencies, and lawyers.
Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research.
4

### Page 5

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
❑ Tokenization records assets or claims as digital tokens on a distributed
ledger (i.e., blockchain), using public or permissioned networks.
❑ Potential benefits: Faster transfers, automated servicing and smaller
investment units.
❑ Shared records can reduce reconciliation, but trading hours, redemption
terms and investor access remain product-specific.
Two issuance models:

❑ On-chain (native): The security or fund share is issued directly on the
distributed ledger/blockchain.
❑ Off-chain (tokenized representation): The underlying asset remains
outside the blockchain, but ownership claims and economic rights are
represented digitally on-chain.
❑ Tokens may be then either transferred to external wallets (distributed) or
platform-bound on the blockchain (represented).
Tokenization can move issuance, settlement and servicing onto a shared ledger

### Page 6

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
Credit is tokenized at wrapper or asset level; most value is still represented
There are two broad approaches to tokenizing credit
❑ Wrapper-level tokenization: The underlying credit assets remain in a
conventional portfolio structure, while a fund or SPV is tokenized, e.g.
Galaxy’s new-issue CLO with debt tranches issued and tokenized on
Avalanche, and Apollo Diversified Credit (ACRED).

❑ Asset-level / native tokenization: Blockchain is integrated into the
underlying credit asset's issuance, ownership record and/or lifecycle.
Examples are Figure Technologies and Tradable, which use blockchains to
support loan administration, ownership records, payments tracking, and
servicing.
❑ Figure can be broken down into distributed vs represented, given it
can be reclassified if they become movable and transferrable.
❑ Asset-level vs wrapper-level shows what is tokenized; distributed vs

represented shows how the token can circulate.
State of tokenized securities
❑ Private credit stands ~$50bn in total (~$8bn distributed vs ~$42bn
represented). Includes asset-backed credit ($33bn), specialty finance
($13bn), corporate credit ($2bn), and diversified credit ($795mn).
❑ Use case is mostly record-keeping; growing adoption for transferable
tokenized credit funds.
❑ Tokenized securitized market is also still mostly at the fund level (i.e. KKR &

Securitize, Hamilton Lane) but efforts to tokenize underlying assets is
growing (i.e. Figure, Centrifuge, Intain). E.g. Figure Technologies is
reportedly prepping its first fully tokenized RMBS issuance in the US for
late 2026/early 2027.

### Page 7

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
Benefits: Transparency – one shared record replaces three reconciled copies
❑ Problem: Traditional securitization can be opaque. Investors often rely on
periodic, manually reconciled reports rather than direct visibility.
❑ Solution: A shared ledger for every party, tokenization allows investors to
access an audit trail time-stamped with evidence of tamper history, visible

holder register to both issuer and regulator, and loan level by default,
making it permissioned.
Potential use case examples
❑ Immutable, public records of the blockchain prevents double pledging of
assets (e.g., First Brands controversy).
❑ Illuminates circular financing (e.g., AI data centre capex buildout), where a
company provides liquidity to a second company so that it can buy its
goods/services.
❑ Commingled cash flows can obscure a company’s operating

performance and can make it more complex for an investor in
evaluating its underlying economic health and cash position.
❑ Enables more bespoke structures (i.e., multi-strategy CDOs for private
credit loan books), by mapping investor exposure to underlying assets,
helping establish correlations and credit risk that may not be immediately
apparent.

### Page 8

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
Benefits: Liquidity – platforms can build a supply-and-demand flywheel
❑ Problem: The structural complexity of the asset class can negatively
impact investor sentiment, serving as a headwind for market growth, and
ultimately impact market depth, liquidity, and prices. And the operational
complexity required for bringing a securitized deal to market can lead to

long settlement times, producing a liquidity gap for an investor.
❑ Solution: The asset transparency resulting from the use of a shared
ledger as core, underlying market infrastructure would help to draw-in
more investors that have traditionally sat on the sidelines. This has been a
hurdle for both structured credit and private credit assets.
❑ A decrease in the perceived complexity cost should naturally lead to a
cohort of net-new investors allocating to the asset class, enabling both

market growth and the added liquidity achieved from a developed
secondary market. An analog, TradFi example of this can be found in the
CLO market, where primary market growth has enabled a robust
secondary market to evolve, further supporting investor sentiment for the
asset class.  The scale achieved has been a factor in driving spreads
tighter over the last few years.
❑ The process simplification enabled by using a block-chain as
foundational infrastructure can lead to time savings, spurring demand

which then attracts greater supply.  Figure Technology’s “Connect”
Platform, a two-sided marketplace developed on the Provenance
blockchain, is an example, connecting loan originators with asset buyers.
Its HELOC offering, which lowers the time and cost required for an
originator to price a loan, has been the service which has played an
outsized role in driving market adoption – Q3 saw growth, with Figure
Connect volume reaching $4.3bn compared to Q2’s $2.8bn.

Connect is an early example of a tokenized securitization marketplace
Sources: Figure Technology Solutions financial filings, AlphaSense,, Deutsche Bank Research.

### Page 9

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
Benefits: Lower costs and faster settlement – ~50–90 bps to bring a deal to market
❑ Problem: Intermediaries (e.g., arranging banks, rating agencies,
lawyers, and trustees) provide essential services for deal activity and
the ongoing administration of cash distributions and collateral
monitoring.
❑ Yet, this costs investors and borrowers fees and processing time,

delaying when an investor receives their first cash distribution by up to
4 months.
❑ Solution: Embedding blockchain into the securitization value chain
could substitute outright (the rails and software providing the service)
or help stakeholders coordinate more efficiently, lowering transaction
costs and time to value for investors. This would also compress
settlement timelines and speed up capital flows.
❑ Net impact: As a distributed ledger, blockchain has features relevant to

securitization: immutable records ensure consistent, reliable data
permeance, and distribution gives participants greater data access.
❑ Embedding blockchain in the securitized value chain would enable four
qualities for the market that would unlock the next stage of growth:
transparency, liquidity, cost efficiency, and speed of settlement.
❑ The savings generated could bring deal costs down materially, by
upwards of 85%.

### Page 10

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
Securitization has supported US growth; blockchain could be the next step
The size of the US Securitized market is ~45% of US GDP❑ Structured credit has been an asset class traditionally associated with
financial innovation: it turns a pool of assets that individually carry lower
credit ratings and more credit risk, into a structured pool whose senior
tranche is often AAA.

❑ And it has been an economic growth driver – at $15trn of debt
outstanding, it is roughly 20% of US nonfinancial debt outstanding, and
just north of 45% of US GDP.
❑ The next inflection point of growth for the market could come from using
blockchain technology to optimize the securitization value chain,
embedding origination, structuring, distribution, and administration on-
chain as programmable, automated services.
❑ The continued growth of stablecoins in the years ahead, in addition to US

Treasury funds will be a necessary precursor for the growth of the
tokenized, securitized markets, as it has been for the Structured credit
markets in the TradFi world.
❑ An important relative value feature that a Structured asset provides is the
yield over US government debt – as more investment flows into the DeFi
space, Tokenized Structured products will see material growth.
❑Ultimately, the market’s expansion would be a tailwind for US GDP
growth.

Sources: RWA.xyz, DefiLlama, MacroMicro, CoinGecko, Deutsche Bank Research.
Sources: SIFMA, FRED (St Louis Federal Reserve), Deutsche Bank Research.

US US SEC stipulates that the tokenized format of credit/structured products does not change how federal securities laws apply to it.
EU
Tokenized securities fall outside MiCA. Falls entirely under EU financial

protection and trading rules).
UK
Digital sandbox, 16 firms are issuing and settling tokenized assets in the
Digital Securities sandbox. A tokenization roadmap is due later this year,
with a custody consultation in H1 2027.
Hong Kong Puts security tokens under the same regulation as traditional securities.
Singapore Project Guardian includes native issuance of asset-backed security and several tokenized private credit funds.
Japan Puts security tokens under the same regulation as traditional securities.

China Banned security tokens on shore, but off-shore tokens can have regulatory approval
❑ The tokenized RWA market remains small ($421bn in Nov 2026)
relative to traditional markets, and token issuance does not
automatically create secondary-market liquidity. Tokenized
securitized debt faces similar challenges to broader tokenized trends.
❑ Secondary-market liquidity remains low. Issuance growth has not
consistently translated into active trading or deep markets.

❑ Regulatory uncertainty: Fragmented or incomplete frameworks slow
institutional adoption and complicate integration with traditional
financial infrastructure. Legal uncertainty persists over whether token
ownership conveys ownership of the underlying asset or a claim on it.
❑ Fragmented infrastructure: Public blockchains, permissioned
institutional ledgers and hybrid platforms often lack interoperability,
fragmenting markets. Clearstream, DTCC and Euroclear published a

joint framework to address this in March 2026.
❑ Limited integration with existing custody, settlement, and record-
keeping arrangements prevents institutions from scaling tokenized
products. Proprietary infrastructure and centralized controls also
raise questions about ownership and governance.

### Page 12

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026 12
Outlook: Tokenization is still infrastructure-led; gains are operational today, credit-risk and liquidity benefits come
later
Tokenized securitization still mostly used for record-keeping purposes; how
much financial activity is adopting blockchain is still small
❑ Tokenization still mostly helps with the infrastructure/plumbing layer of the
securitized market: data, servicing, settlement and collateral mobility.

❑ Hence, distributed value of the tokenized securitized market ($8bn) remains
small compared to represented ($42bn).
❑ In contrast, US Treasury debt remains the largest (ex-stablecoins) on-chain
tokenized asset segment reaching $14bn (distributed) in November 2026.
❑ Demand reflects appetite for liquid, short-term, risk-free collateral, that can
generate yield unlike illiquid segments like private credit.
Potential to transform credit risk, but increased liquidity is further out

❑ Industry is currently in the infrastructure phase. Gains are so far operational:
lower costs, faster settlement times and data transparency.
❑ Move towards tokenizing underlying assets (rather than fund-wrapper style)
has potential to effectively reduce credit risk by countering double-pledging,
settlement risks.
❑ Tokenization remains concentrated among a small number of issuers and lacks
a deep, and liquid secondary market. Distributed value is still small, illustrating

limited activity in actual borrowing demand.
❑ Near term, tokenized Treasuries and money market funds will be the first
market to scale. Longer-term opportunities for private credit, once efforts to
integrate tokenized infrastructure with legacy systems (i.e. DTCC, NYSE,
Nasdaq), money (stablecoins, tokenized deposits), and broader regulation
progresses.

Document 476: World Outlook_ 2027_ Great Expectations, but Risky Business (2026-11-24)

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

World Outlook

1 
Authors 
Jim Reid 
Global Head of Macro and 
Thematic Research

David Folkerts-Landau, Ph.D. 
Group Chief Economist &  
Global Head of Research

November 24, 2026 
2027: Great Expectations, but Risky Business 
Great Expectations, but Risky Business feels an appropriate title for our 
2027 World Outlook, especially after last week’s savage equity sell-off. We 
remain firmly of the view that AI will have a significant productivity impact, 
and the evidence is becoming more supportive: AI-related capex is already 
large enough to influence US growth, while early signs point to stronger 
productivity in more AI-intensive sectors. History nevertheless warns that 
capex-led technological revolutions rarely travel smoothly to the promised 
land. Overinvestment, busts and unexpected winners and losers are part of 
the journey. 
Geopolitics could again be responsible for some of the largest forecast 
errors. Pressure for a Russia-Ukraine ceasefire may increase, but European 
security challenges will persist; US-China competition is likely to intensify 
across strategic technologies; and the Middle East remains an important 
source of energy risk. 
That backdrop matters for bonds. Our rates strategists are now broadly 
neutral on duration, while expecting steeper yield curves. Rising government 
supply, large fiscal demands and vulnerability to supply shocks will should 
keep equilibrium rates and term premia structurally above 2010s norms. 
Overall, the same forces producing the upside are also creating the risks. AI 
could deliver the productivity breakthrough the developed world has been 
waiting decades for, but the investment required to get there creates the 
possibility of overinvestment. Fiscal activism can support growth, but adds 
to bond supply and debt. Greater strategic autonomy can boost resilience, 
but requires huge spending on defence, energy and technology, while 
adding to geopolitical tensions. 
If our great expectations are fulfilled, 2027 could ultimately be a very good 
year. But don't expect it to feel like that every day in what is likely to be 
another volatile year. 
Full report available for clients of Deutsche Bank Research here: World 
Outlook 2027: Great Expectations, but Risky Business

2
《消費市場後續觀察的幾個關鍵點》
關鍵一：服務撐盤、商品降溫，整體動能由加速轉為高檔
整理。 服務（含餐飲）貢獻 2.5pp（9月 2.7pp），零售
（不含汽油與餐飲）貢獻 1.2pp（9月 1.6pp、夏季高點
約 2.0pp）。零售降幅大於服務，顯示家計先縮減商品、
保留體驗型支出。
關鍵二：消費動力來源已由「財富效應」向「薪資所得」
移動。 低所得稅後薪資 +4.7%、中所得略低於 4%、高

所得 +3.4%，薪資成長呈「反K」。低所得總支出因此領
先；頂端5% 則仍仰賴資產價格，其支出由 +5.9% 降至
+5.7%。薪資驅動型消費較不受股市波動影響，但對就業
市場較敏感。
關鍵三：支出成長連5個月高於稅後薪資，缺口靠存款、
信用與財富填補。 BofA 認為此主要為基期效應（薪資絕
對金額遠大於卡片支出）。我們接受此解釋的方向，但指
出存款已不再增加、最低還款戶與401(k)困難提領上升，
緩衝正在邊際消耗。此為本期最需追蹤的結構性變化。
關鍵四：年底檔期「量增、價降、線上化」。 假期品項
支出明顯高於2025年，線上高出整體約 3pp；平均客單
價低於2025年，耐久品交易筆數 YoY 下降。對通路而言，
有利折價與電商，不利高單價耐久品與專業服飾。尚需留
意前置消費風險，但問卷顯示多數受訪者傾向在季後段購
物。
資料來源：美國銀行 2026/11/12，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/24
10月多數零售與服務之非必需品類成長放緩

### Page 3

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要
零售客流量表
• 美國家計消費成長由「全面擴
散」轉為「集中」
• 消費引擎已由財富效應轉向薪
資所得
• 「支出持續高於薪資」與「頂
端5% 財富效應反轉」同時發
生，將削弱消費的兩大支柱。
3
《兩大支柱與風險》
美國家計消費仍屬「穩健」，10月每戶卡片支出 YoY
+5.8%，不含汽油 +4.6%，分別較9月放緩 40bps 與
50bps，成長主軸未變，與上月最大差異在於成長由「全
面擴散」轉為「集中」。10月多數品類放緩，僅無店鋪零
售 （ +13.5% ） 與 雜 項 商 店 （ +8.4% ） 加速， 服飾僅
+1.0%。年底檔期呈現相同特徵：假期品項支出高於去年，
線上高出整體約 3pp，但平均客單價低於2025年，耐久

品交易筆數年減。消費者仍在花錢，但轉向線上、價值通
路與低單價品項，屬「以量換價」，並非升級消費。
消費引擎已由財富效應轉向薪資所得。低所得稅後薪資成
長 +4.7%，為各族群最高，帶動其總支出成長 +5.9%，
首度超越頂端5%（+5.7%）。薪資成長呈「反K」，使
2025年的K型分化在支出面大致收斂。此結構較不受股市
波動影響，但對就業市場更為敏感。兩大支柱與風險：目
前消費由兩根支柱支撐，一是中低所得的薪資所得，二是
頂端5% 的財富效應。前者的隱憂在於，各族群支出成長
已連續5個月高於稅後薪資，差距約 1.2~1.9pp。考量基
期效應，此差距尚不代表入不敷出。但存款已不再增加，
最低還款戶比重與401(k)困難提領同步上升，顯示邊際家
戶正在動用緩衝。
後者的隱憂在於，頂端5% 的支出與薪資缺口最大，幾乎
全賴資產增值支撐，且該族群在住宿、航空、家具等品類
的支出占比偏高。兩根支柱目前互為備援。若兩者同時轉
弱，將沒有族群能抵銷，這是最需防範的情境；惟目前兩
項風險僅屬邊際跡象。

資料來源：美國銀行 2026/11/12，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/24
近五個月各所得族群支出成長皆高於稅後薪資成長

重點摘要
零售客流量表
• 3Q26 美股核心消費財報呈現
明確的「K 型分化」
• 3Q26財報彙整五大主軸：來客
數、IEEPA關稅、高油價、核心
消費量縮、財測方向兩極化
4
《核心消費產業 3Q26 財報彙整》
3Q26 美股核心消費財報呈現明確的「K 型分化」_以價值
與流量取勝的量販／折扣通路全面超預期並上修財測，價
格敏感的速食、北美包裝食品飲料與品牌修正股則持續承
壓。
本季五大主軸：
本季美股核心消費財報的第一個主軸，是來客數成為績效
分水嶺。Target 同店成長 5.6%，其中來客貢獻 5.0%；
Ross 同店成長 8% 同樣以來客為主要驅動，Costco 全球
來客亦增加 3.3%。相對地，麥當勞美國來客轉為負成長，

Home Depot 交易筆數仍下滑 0.5%，顯示家居與速食業
者主要倚賴客單價支撐營收。
第二，IEEPA 關稅退款顯著墊高帳面獲利。最高法院於
2026 年 2 月裁定 IEEPA 關稅違法後，退款於第二、三季
陸續入帳：Target 本季 EPS 含 0.50 美元退款，Costco
含 0.15 美元，Lululemon 前一季含 0.86 美元，PepsiCo
核心營業利益約有 4 個百分點來自退款。Lowe’s 則選擇
將退款再投資於價格。剔除退款後，多數零售商 EPS 成長
回落至中個位數至低雙位數，並墊高 2027 年的比較基期。
第三，高油價與升息持續壓抑中低所得族群。10 月美國
汽油均價創下每加侖 4.31 美元新高；聯準會 9 月升息至
3.75%～4.00% 後，10 月按兵不動；30 年期房貸利率約
7.18%，成屋市場陷入停滯。
第四，必需消費品呈現「量回價退」，但北美仍是弱點。
P&G 有機成長 4.0%，其中 2.4 個百分點來自銷量，
Mondelēz 與 Colgate 亦恢復以量驅動。然而 Colgate 北

美有機銷售衰退 1.6%，PepsiCo 北美飲料量下滑 2%，
Kimberly-Clark 有機銷售衰退 1.3%。
第五，財測以上修為主，但結構分化。Walmart、Target、
TJX、Coca-Cola 與 Colgate 上修全年展望；Lowe’s 上
修營收卻下修利潤率，PepsiCo 下修核心 EPS 成長，
Nike 與 Lululemon 則持續下修財測。

5
《大型量販與折扣零售表現最佳的子產業》
本季表現最佳的子產業：來客數、電商與會員制三重飛輪
共振，全數上修或維持財測，預期量販／折扣通路在
4Q26~1H27 維持市佔擴張；主要觀察點為假期季促銷強
度（毛利率）、汽油價格對名目同店的支撐是否快速消退，
以及 2027 年剔除退款後的 EPS 基期。
資料來源：各家財報數彙整 2026/11/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，
2026/11/24
同店成長的排序幾乎就是來客數的排序
《必需消費品_北美量能仍衰退、成本通膨侵蝕毛利率》
全球層面「量回價退」的健康轉變已確立，但北美量能仍
衰退、成本通膨侵蝕毛利率；國際曝險高、產品創新力強

者勝出。
2027必需消費品的核心議題將轉向「量的持續性」與
「毛利率保衛」。油價與運費對包材、物流成本形成壓力；
若油價回落則為毛利率上行風險。對北美包裝食品仍保守
（GLP-1 滲透與私有品牌分食），偏好 KO、PG 等量價
均衡、國際分散度高的龍頭。

《品牌服飾與鞋類仍保守》
品牌商仍處於「庫存與通路清理→營收衰退→利潤率重建」
的早中期，財測仍在下修；美國本土雖有企穩，但中國與
生活風格品類拖累未止。 產業內的贏家是通路（TJX、
ROST 承接庫存），而非品牌。關稅退款為品牌商帶來一
次性現金，但未來仍面臨替代性關稅措施的成本基礎。品
牌動能自生活風格向專業運動轉移（On、Hoka 等挑戰者
持續分食），無利潤率重建時程明朗化前維持減碼。反向
訊號：NKE 北美批發訂單加速、中國庫存觸底。
《餐飲個股分化_外食減少》
美國 QSR 來客持續負成長，價值戰升溫；結構性轉型標
的（Starbucks）與國際加盟模式（MCD IDL、KFC）是

本季亮點。 低所得客群受高油價擠壓最直接，外食頻率
為最先被削減的支出。 2027 年初將進入更激烈的價值競
爭（MCD 全國入門方案、Taco Bell $3 以下菜單），產
業利潤率受壓。偏好具備自身轉型催化劑或國際單位成長
的標的（SBUX、YUM），對美國來客曝險高且成本通膨
未緩者保守。

Document 535: トランプ政権に逆風、AI・半導体株の勝ち組は変わるのか ～米中間選挙で揺らぐAI投資と個人投資家の選択～

Source PDF: all report pdf/トランプ政権に逆風、AI・半導体株の勝ち組は変わるのか ～米中間選挙で揺らぐAI投資と個人投資家の選択～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026年11月 16日

1/6 
トランプ政権に逆風、AI・半導体株の勝ち組は変わ
るのか 
～米中間選挙で揺らぐ AI投資と個人投資家の選択～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  柏村 祐

（要旨） 
○ 2026年 11月の米中間選挙における民主党の躍進（共和党の敗北）を受け、AI・半導体株が
急落している。しかし、半導体株の調整は選挙前からすでに始まっており、巨額の AI投資に
対する収益化への疑念に、政治的・規制的な不確実性が加わった結果とみるべきである。 
○ 中間選挙を経て、AI開発の物理的基盤であるデータセンターに対する規制強化が新たなリス
クとして浮上している。米国では地域社会の反対などにより、データセンターの開発停止措
置（モラトリアム）が急増しており、インフラ制約が成長シナリオのボトルネックとなりつ
つある。 
○ 政治的逆風やインフラ制約が意識されるなか、今後の AI・半導体株の選別において重要性が
一段と高まる指標は「利益成長力」である。2027年の予想 EPS修正率を見ると、半導体・ハ
ードウェアといった上流工程の利益予想は大きく上方修正されている一方、巨大クラウド企
業や電力関連では上方修正幅が小さい。 
○ 個人投資家にとっては、「AI銘柄の全面高」から利益成長力に基づく選別が重要な局面へと
移りつつあることを認識し、ポートフォリオを再点検することが求められる。期待先行の銘
柄から利益成長の裏付けを持つ銘柄へのシフトと、規制リスクの直撃を受けるセクターの再
点検が、実務上の対応策となるだろう。

1. 共和党敗北の衝撃とAI株の動揺 
2026年11月の米中間選挙は、金融市場に大きな衝撃を与えた。民主党が予想以上の規模で躍進
し、トランプ大統領率いる共和党が上下両院で敗北を喫したことで、金融市場では政治・規制環
境の変化に対する警戒感が高まった。とりわけ投資家の関心を集めているのが、これまで相場を
牽引してきたAI（人工知能）・半導体株の急落である。 
中間選挙直後の11月4日、米国株式市場はいったん上昇したものの、翌 5日には一転して急落
した。なかでもAIインフラを支える半導体やデータセンター関連銘柄の下げ幅は際立っていた。
次期議会で主導権を握る民主党が、データセンターの建設規制や巨大テック企業への監視を強化

### Page 2

2/6 
するとの懸念が広がったことも、下落の背景にあるとみられる。 
マクロ環境の不確実性が高まり、ボラティリティ（価格変動）が高まるなか、個人投資家は「A
Iテーマ」とどう向き合い、ポートフォリオをどう再構築すべきなのか。 
本稿では、選挙前後の株価動向、データセンター規制、業種別の利益予想の 3点をデータで確
認し、個人投資家の資産運用上の論点を整理する。

2. 共和党敗北とAI・半導体株の現在地 
(1) 中間選挙後、AI・半導体株の下落は加速したのか 
2026年11月の米中間選挙における民主党の躍進（共和党の敗北）は、株式市場、とりわけAI・
半導体株に大きな波紋を広げた。選挙直後の 11月 5日（木曜日）、米国の主要株価指数は急落
し、ナスダック総合指数は－5.5％、S&P500種株価指数は－4.4％の下落を記録した。特に半導体
やデータセンター関連銘柄の下落幅は、市場全体を大きく上回るものとなった。民主党が主導権
を握る新たな政治シナリオのもとで、AIインフラ構築に対する規制強化や監視の目が厳しくなる
との懸念が、投資家心理を冷やしたとみられる。 
しかし、この「AI株安」を単なる選挙結果のショックとして片付けるべきではない。State 
Street Investment Managementの分析によれば、AI関連株の調整は中間選挙の前からすでに始ま
っていた。事実、世界の半導体株は選挙前の段階で、直近の高値から約20％の下落（ドローダウ
ン）を経験していた（資料 1）。巨額のAI設備投資に対するリターン（収益化）への疑念や、高止
まりする業績期待に対するハードルがすでに高まっていたところに、中間選挙による政治的・規
制的な不確実性が加わり、既存の価格調整（リプライシング）が加速したとみるのが自然であろ
う。

### Page 3

3/6 
資料 1 過去 5年間の半導体株の下落率（ドローダウン） 
（注）緑線は世界の半導体株（MSCI AC World Semiconductor & Semiconductor Equipment Industry）、青面は米国株全体
（S&P500）の直近高値からの下落率（％）を示す。 
（出所）State Street Investment Management, “Midterms add to an AI correction already underway”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所作成

(2) データセンター規制はAI・半導体株の新たなリスクになるのか 
中間選挙を経て新たなリスクとして注目されているのが、AI開発の物理的基盤であるデータセ
ンターに対する規制強化である。米国では選挙前から電力需要の逼迫や環境負荷を懸念する地域
社会の反対が強まっており、民主党の躍進によって、こうした懸念が政策に反映される可能性が
高まっている。 
IEA（国際エネルギー機関）の報告書によれば、米国におけるデータセンターの開発停止措置
（モラトリアム）は急増している。2026年 1～7月に導入された開発停止措置の件数は、2025年
通年の約5倍に達した（資料 2）。地域住民からの反対理由は、土地利用や水資源・環境への影
響、電力価格の高騰懸念など多岐にわたる。こうした地域社会の反対を背景に、2024年 1月から
2026年5月半ばまでの間に、米国で開発が阻止、撤回、または停滞したAIデータセンター事業は
46件に上り、これらの事業に関連する発表ベースの投資額は合計 1,700億ドルを超えている。AI
ブームの足元で、物理的なインフラ制約と規制リスクが、関連企業の成長シナリオに対する明確
なボトルネックとして浮上している。

### Page 4

4/6 
資料 2 米国における懸念事項別のデータセンター開発停止措置 
（注）米国における州、郡、市町村レベルでのデータセンター開発停止措置（モラトリアム）の件数。複数の懸念事項が挙げられて
いる場合は均等に分割して集計。2026年は 1～7月のデータ。 
（出所）IEA, “Powering Artificial Intelligence”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(3) AI・半導体株の勝ち組は利益成長力で決まるのか 
政治的逆風やインフラ制約が意識されるなか、今後のAI・半導体株の選別において、重要性が
一段と高まるのが「利益成長力」である。マクロ環境の不確実性が高まる局面では、期待先行で
買われた銘柄は淘汰され、確かな業績の裏付けを持つ企業に資金が集中しやすい。 
J.P. Morgan Asset Managementの分析によれば、AIバリューチェーンのなかでも業種によって
将来の利益成長期待に明確な濃淡が表れている。2026年10月 29日時点における2027年の予想EP
S（1株当たり利益）について、2025年末からの修正率を見ると、半導体・半導体製造装置（Semi
s）が＋14.6％と突出して高い上方修正を示している（資料 3）。次いで、通信機器などのハードウ
ェアが＋4.5％となっている。一方で、AI投資を牽引する巨大クラウド企業（Hyperscalers）は＋
1.5％、ソフトウェアは＋0.6％にとどまり、電力関連（Power）に至っては 0.0％と上方修正が見
られない。 
これは、AIの最終的な需要（エンドユーザーへの普及や収益化）には依然として不透明感が残
るものの、その手前にある「計算資源の確保」という物理的な制約を解消するための上流工程
（半導体やハードウェア）については、強い需要が見込まれていることを示している。中間選挙
後の新たな市場環境において、個人投資家にはAIテーマをひと括りにするのではなく、規制リス
クに加え、利益成長の持続性や株価の割高感を見極めながら、「真の勝ち組」を選別する視点が求
められている。

### Page 5

5/6

資料 3 2026年および 2027年の予想 EPS修正率（業種別） 
（注）2025年 12月末時点からのコンセンサス予想 EPS（1株当たり利益）の修正率。巨大クラウド企業（Hyperscalers）は
Amazon、Alphabet、Meta、Microsoft、Oracleの加重平均。電力関連は電気機器および電力公益事業の加重平均。 
（出所）J.P. Morgan Asset Management, “3Q26 Earnings Bulletin”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

3. 「AI株選別時代」の資産防衛 
第 2章で見てきたように、AI・半導体株を取り巻く環境は「期待先行の全面高」から、規制リ
スクと利益成長力が綱引きをする「選別のフェーズ」へと移りつつある。選挙前から広がってい
たデータセンターの開発停止措置（モラトリアム）に加え、民主党の躍進による規制強化への警
戒感が高まる一方で、半導体やハードウェアといった上流工程の利益成長期待は引き続き根強
い。 
ただし、これはAIブームの終わりを意味しない。個人投資家に求められるのは、第 1章で提起
した通り、新たな環境に合わせてポートフォリオを再構築することである。実務上の対応策とし
て、以下の2点が挙げられる。 
第一に、これまでの期待先行から実需（利益成長）へのシフトである。これまではAIというテ
ーマ性だけで買われていた銘柄も多かったが、今後は業績による選別が進むと考えられる。デー
タセンター規制などの影響を見極めつつ、需要と利益成長の裏付けを持つ半導体や通信インフラ
関連のハードウェア企業へ、投資の比重を移すことが有効な選択肢となる。J.P. Morgan Asset 
Managementのデータが示す通り、2027年の利益予想が大きく上方修正されている領域が候補とな
る。ただし、上方修正がすでに株価に織り込まれていないかは銘柄ごとに確認する必要がある。 
第二に、規制リスクの直撃を受けるセクターの再点検である。AI投資の恩恵を受けると期待さ
れてきた電力や一部の不動産（データセンターREITなど）は、地域社会の反対や環境規制の強化
によって、想定通りの成長シナリオを描けなくなるリスク（投資回収の遅れや資産価値の低下に
つながるリスク）が浮上している。これらの銘柄を保有している場合は、政策動向を注視し、必

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
要に応じてウェイトを引き下げるなどのリスク管理が必要になる。 
同じ AI関連でも、2027年の予想EPS修正率は半導体・半導体製造装置の＋14.6％から電力関連
の0.0％まで開いている。加えて、地域社会の反対を背景に阻止、撤回、または停滞したAIデー
タセンター事業は46件、発表ベースの投資額で 1,700億ドル超に上る。中間選挙後は、利益予想
が切り上がっているか、規制の影響を受けやすい事業かという 2点で保有銘柄を点検すること
が、個人投資家の資産防衛の出発点になるだろう。

### Page 1

World Trends

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/5 
メキシコ中銀は４会合ぶりの利下げ実施も、「一度限りの調整」を強調 
～インフレ懸念が残るなかで長期の金利据え置きも、ペソ相場は上値の重い展開が続くか～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ メキシコ銀行（中銀）は11 月５日の定例会合で、政策金利を 25bp 引き下げて 6.25％とする
ことを決定した。４会合ぶりの利下げは市場予想に反するものであったものの、中銀は今回
の決定を「一度限りの調整」とし、先行きは長期間にわたって金利を据え置く方針を示した。
ＦＲＢは金融引き締め姿勢を維持している一方、メキシコ中銀は景気・物価動向を重視した
独自の政策判断を継続している。 
○ 足元のメキシコ経済は、ＡＩ・半導体関連需要を背景とする輸出拡大などを追い風に持ち直
している。しかし、先行きには米国の通商政策を巡る不透明感などを理由に、下振れリスク
が残る。一方で、足元のインフレ率には再び加速する兆しが出ており、基調的な物価上昇圧
力も根強いことから、先行きの追加利下げのハードルは高いと考えられる。 
○ 先行きの金融政策は、物価や景気動向に加え、ＦＲＢの政策運営やペソ相場を見極める様子
見姿勢が続くと見込まれる。ペソ相場については、国内景気の持ち直しや中銀の慎重姿勢が
下支えとなる一方、米国との金利差縮小や USMCA の再交渉を巡る不透明感などが重しとなる
展開が見込まれる。このため、当面のペソ相場は上値の重い展開が続くと予想される。

### Page 2

2/5 
【メキシコ中銀は４会合ぶりに「一度限りの」利下げ決定、政策金利は４年半ぶりの低水準に】 
メキシコ銀行（中央銀行）は、11 月５日に開催した定例の金融政策決定会合において、政策金利
である翌日物銀行間金利の誘導目標を
25bp 引き下げ、6.25％とすることを決定
した（図１）。同行は、2025 年から金融
緩和を進めてきたものの、今年５月の定
例会合での利下げを最後に、６月、８月、
９月は３会合連続で政策金利を 6.50％
に据え置いた。このため、今回の決定は
４会合ぶりの利下げとなる。今回の決定
は、５人の政策委員のうちロドリゲス総
裁ら３人の賛成による多数決で採択さ
れたものの、残る２人は金利の据え置き
を主張するなど、金融政策を巡る意見の相違も明らかとなった。市場の事前予想では据え置き予想
が大勢を占めていたため、今回の決定は予想外の利下げとなった。 
会合後に公表された声明文では、今回の利下げについて「一度限りの調整（ajuste puntual）」と
位置付けるとともに、先行きについて「政策金利を現行水準で長期間にわたり据え置くことが、イ
ンフレの目標への収束、対外的なマクロ経済が招く課題への対応の観点でも適切」との考えを明示
した。このことは、今回の決定が継続的な金融緩和局面への回帰を意味するものではなく、国内の
景気や物価動向を踏まえて金融引き締めの程度を微調整する性格の強いものであったことを示唆
していると考えられる。

【FRBは金融引き締め姿勢を維持する一方、メキシコ中銀は「独自の政策判断」を継続する意向】 
今回の利下げ決定を巡っては、米国とメキシコの金融政策の方向性が異なっていることが注目さ
れる。ＦＲＢ（米連邦準備制度理事会）は、９月のFOMC（連邦公開市場委員会）において、政策金
利（ＦＦ金利）を 25bp 引き上げて 3.75～4.00％とすることを決定した。10 月会合では据え置いた
ものの、米国経済の底堅さや物価上昇圧力の根強さを背景に、金融引き締め姿勢を維持している。
一方、メキシコ中銀は９月の定例会合で政策金利を 6.50％に据え置くとともに、先行きの政策運営
については、両国のマクロ経済環境の違いを理由に、ＦＲＢによる政策金利の調整に機械的に反応
する必要はないとの考えを示していた（注１）。今回発表された声明文では、９月会合の声明文で示
された前述の文言は繰り返されなかった。しかし、利下げに動いたことで、両国の経済環境の違い
を踏まえた独自の政策判断を実行に移したと捉えられる。 
こうした判断の背景には、米国では堅調な内需が物価上昇圧力を招いている一方、メキシコでは

注１ ９月25 日付レポート「メキシコ中銀、政策判断の自由度を重視」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666044.html）

### Page 3

3/5 
景気回復が進んでいるものの、経済全体に需給の緩みが残っているという違いがある。今回の声明
文でも、「ディスインフレプロセスが続いている」と評価するとともに、「需給の緩みを勘案すれば、
ペソ安による物価への影響は限定的」、「需要インフレ圧力は認められず、これまで実施してきた金
融引き締めの効果も持続している」との判断を示した。このため、ＦＲＢが追加利上げに動いた場
合でも、メキシコ中銀が直ちに政策金利を引き上げる必要性は低いと考えられる。しかし、両国の
金利差の縮小がペソ安の動きを加速させる場合には、その判断は難しくなるであろう。

【メキシコ経済は持ち直しの動きを強めるも、内需の先行きには不透明感が残る】 
足元のメキシコ経済には、持ち直しの動きが確認されている。７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成長率（速報
値）は、前年同期比＋3.2％と前期（同＋
2.1％）から伸びが加速した（図２）。前
期比も＋0.9％となり、年率換算ベースで
は＋3.67％と２四半期連続のプラス成長
となっている。分野別の生産動向につい
ても、サービス業や鉱業・製造業で生産
拡大の動きが続くなど、景気回復の動き
を下支えしている。さらに、世界的なＡ
Ｉ（人工知能）・半導体関連需要の旺盛な
動きを反映して、米国向けを中心にコン
ピュータなど自動車以外の輸出が大幅に
拡大しており、外需の堅調さが足元の景気回復を後押ししている。 
ただし、先行きの景気動向には不透明感が残る。トランプ米政権による政策運営の不透明さを理
由に、企業の設備投資には不安定な動きがみられる。USMCA（米国・メキシコ・カナダ協定）の見直
し交渉については、米国とメキシコの間では比較的良好な動きがみられるものの、先行きの動向を
注視する必要性は変わらない。中銀も、声明文では「７-９月の景気拡大が続いた」とする一方、
「見通し期間を通じて需給の緩みが残り、景気を巡るリスクは下振れ方向に傾いている」との見方
を維持している。このため、足元の景気が持ち直しの動きをみせていることを理由に、金融引き締
めの継続が必要と判断する状況にはないと考えられる。

【インフレ率に再び加速する兆し、基調的なインフレ圧力の根強さを反映した動きも】 
さらに、足元のインフレには再加速の動きがみられる。９月のインフレ率は前年比＋3.45％と中
銀の目標レンジ（３±１％）内で推移し
ているものの（図３）、前月（同＋3.26％）
から加速した。さらに、10 月前半のイン
フレ率も前年比＋3.62％となっており、
物価上昇圧力が再び強まっている。背景
には、エネルギーや農産物など変動の大
きい品目で物価上昇圧力が強まっている
ことがある。一方、コアインフレ率は９
月に前年同月比＋3.75％と前月（同＋
3.88％）から鈍化し、10 月前半も同＋
3.78％と半月次ベースでは小幅な鈍化傾
向を維持している。ただし、サービス物価を中心に高い伸びが続いていることから、基調的なイン
フレ圧力は依然として根強いと考えられる。 
このため、中銀は声明文において、2026 年 10-12 月のインフレ見通しを＋3.7％（←＋3.5％）、
コ ア イ ン フ レ 見 通 し も ＋3.7 ％ （ ← ＋
3.6％）に上方修正するなど、短期的にイ
ンフレが上振れするとの見方を示してい
る（図４）。一方で、従来通り 2027 年 10-
12 月にはインフレ率が目標（３％）に収
束するとの見方を維持した（図４）。その
うえで、物価の上振れ要因に「①コアイ
ンフレの高止まり、②ペソ安、③通商政
策や地政学リスクによるインフレ圧力、
④異常気象の影響、⑤コスト上昇圧力」
を挙げ、先行きのインフレリスクは依然
として「上振れ方向に傾いている」と判断している。このため、今回の利下げは、インフレ懸念が
完全に払拭されたことによるものではなく、経済に残る需給の緩みと、金融引き締めの効果が持続
していることを重視した判断と捉えられる。

【長期にわたる様子見が想定されるも、金利差縮小などがペソ相場の重しとなる可能性は高い】 
先行きの金融政策については、前述の通り、政策金利を長期間にわたって現行水準に維持する方
針が示されたことを勘案すれば、様子見姿勢を維持する可能性が高いと考えられる。コアインフレ
率は依然として目標（３％）を上回っているうえ、今回の利下げ決定に２人の政策委員が反対した
ことを勘案すれば、追加利下げの実施には、インフレの一段の鈍化や景気失速など、金融緩和の必

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
要性を裏付ける材料が不可欠となろう。 
さらに、先行きの政策運営を左右する重要な要素となるのは、ＦＲＢの政策運営やペソ相場の動
向であろう。仮にＦＲＢが追加利上げに踏み切れば、メキシコとの政策金利差はさらに縮小し、ペ
ソ相場への下押し圧力が強まる可能性がある。ペソ安による輸入物価の押し上げがインフレに波及
すれば、中銀が政策金利を据え置く期間が一段と長期化することが予想される。一方で、米国の景
気減速による輸出の下押しなどで同国経済が大きく下振れし、インフレも想定以上に鈍化すれば、
追加利下げが検討される余地が生じる。とはいえ、今回の中銀の決定からは、物価安定とペソ相場
の動向を見極める姿勢を重視している様子がうかがえ、利下げのハードルは高いと見込まれる。 
金融市場では、今回の利下げ決定を受けてペソ安圧力が強まった（図５）。背景には、事前の市場
予想では据え置きが優勢であったこと
に加え、米国との政策金利差縮小によ
り、高金利通貨であるペソを買い持ちす
るキャリートレードの魅力が低下した
ことがある。一方で、中銀が長期にわた
って様子見姿勢を維持する考えを示し
たことは、ペソ安の動きに一定の歯止め
をかけることが期待される。先行きにつ
いては、金利差縮小による下押し圧力
と、国内景気の持ち直しによる下支え要
因の綱引きが予想される。とはいえ、
USMCA の再交渉を巡る不透明感、金融市場の不安定化、ＦＲＢの追加利上げが意識されれば、ペソ
安圧力が強まる懸念は残る。このため、当面のペソ相場は上値の重い展開が続くと見込まれる。

主要產業總評：美股待估值合理 
投資研究部
